4. 曲线y=5x+8lnx在点(1,5)处的切线方程为（ ）
A. y=3x+2
B. y=5x
C. y=8x-3
D. y=13x-8

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
对曲线求导：

\[
y'=5+\frac{8}{x}
\]

在点 \((1,5)\) 处，斜率为：

\[
y'(1)=5+\frac{8}{1}=13
\]

切线方程为：

\[
y-5=13(x-1)
\]

即：

\[
y=13x-8
\]

所以答案是：

\[
\boxed{D}
\]
Reasoning

6. 已知函数f(x)=\frac{x+2}{e^x+a}的最大值为1，则a = （ ）
A. \frac{1}{2}
B. 1
C. \frac{3}{2}
D. 2

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
选项中的 \(a\) 均为正数，所以 \(e^x+a>0\)。

若最大值为 \(1\)，则对任意 \(x\) 有

\[
\frac{x+2}{e^x+a}\le 1
\]

即

\[
a\ge x+2-e^x
\]

令

\[
g(x)=x+2-e^x
\]

则

\[
g'(x)=1-e^x
\]

所以 \(g(x)\) 在 \(x=0\) 处取得最大值：

\[
g(0)=0+2-1=1
\]

因此必须有 \(a\ge1\)。

又因为最大值 \(1\) 能取到，所以存在 \(x\) 使

\[
a=x+2-e^x=g(x)\le1
\]

故

\[
a=1
\]

验证：当 \(a=1\) 时，

\[
f(x)\le1 \iff e^x\ge x+1
\]

成立，且 \(f(0)=1\)。

\[
\boxed{\text{B. }1}
\]

10. 在空间中，A、B为两个定点，动点C到直线AB的距离为2，动点D到直线AB的距离为1。若二面角C - AB - D为60°，则（ ）
A. \angle CAD \ge 60^\circ
B. CD \ge \sqrt{3}
C. 当AB \perp CD时，CD \perp平面ABD
D. 当AB \perp平面ACD时，AC \perp AD

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设直线 \(AB\) 为 \(x\) 轴，取 \(C\) 到 \(AB\) 的垂足为原点，令  
\[
C=(0,2,0),\quad A=(a,0,0),\quad B=(b,0,0).
\]
因 \(D\) 到 \(AB\) 的距离为 \(1\)，且二面角 \(C-AB-D=60^\circ\)，可设  
\[
D=\left(t,\frac12,\frac{\sqrt3}{2}\right).
\]

A 项：取 \(a=1,t=0\) 时条件均满足，但  
\[
\cos\angle CAD=\frac{2}{\sqrt{10}}>\frac12,
\]
所以 \(\angle CAD<60^\circ\)，A 错。

B 项：
\[
CD^2=t^2+\left(2-\frac12\right)^2+\left(\frac{\sqrt3}{2}\right)^2
=t^2+3\ge 3,
\]
故 \(CD\ge \sqrt3\)，B 对。

C 项：若 \(AB\perp CD\)，则 \(t=0\)。此时  
\[
\vec{CD}=\left(0,-\frac32,\frac{\sqrt3}{2}\right),
\]
它与 \(\vec{AB}\) 和 \(\vec{AD}\) 都垂直，因此 \(CD\perp\) 平面 \(ABD\)，C 对。

D 项：若 \(AB\perp\) 平面 \(ACD\)，则 \(A,C,D\) 的 \(x\) 坐标相同，得 \(a=t=0\)。于是  
\[
\vec{AC}=(0,2,0),\quad \vec{AD}=\left(0,\frac12,\frac{\sqrt3}{2}\right),
\]
数量积不为 \(0\)，故 \(AC\) 不垂直于 \(AD\)，D 错。

因此答案为 \(\boxed{BC}\)。

Prompt
请解答以下数学题（多选题）。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

11. 已知圆C_1:(x+1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_2:(x-1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_3:x^2+(y-\sqrt{3})^2=1，直线l:y=kx+b与C_1, C_2, C_3均有两个交点。记l被C_1, C_2, C_3截得的弦长分别为s_1、s_2、s_3，则（ ）
A. k可以取任意实数
B. 满足s_1=s_2=s_3的直线l共有3条
C. 满足s_1+s_2+s_3=3的直线l多于3条
D. 当b=0时，s_1+s_2+s_3的最大值为\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设三圆圆心分别为  
\[
O_1(-1,0),\quad O_2(1,0),\quad O_3(0,\sqrt3),
\]
直线 \(l:kx-y+b=0\)，则
\[
d_1=\frac{|b-k|}{\sqrt{1+k^2}},\quad
d_2=\frac{|b+k|}{\sqrt{1+k^2}},\quad
d_3=\frac{|b-\sqrt3|}{\sqrt{1+k^2}}.
\]
弦长
\[
s_i=2\sqrt{1-d_i^2}.
\]

**A 选项**：取 \(k=\frac1{\sqrt3}\)，则 \(\sqrt{1+k^2}=\frac2{\sqrt3}\)。  
由前两个圆的相交条件可得
\[
b\in\left(-\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac1{\sqrt3}\right),
\]
而由第三个圆可得
\[
b\in\left(\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac5{\sqrt3}\right),
\]
两区间无交集，所以不存在这样的 \(b\)。故 A 错。

**B 选项**：  
\(s_1=s_2=s_3\) 等价于
\[
(b-k)^2=(b+k)^2=(b-\sqrt3)^2.
\]
由 \((b-k)^2=(b+k)^2\) 得 \(bk=0\)。

- 若 \(b=0\)，则 \(|k|=\sqrt3\)，得 2 条直线；
- 若 \(k=0\)，则 \(|b|=|b-\sqrt3|\)，得 \(b=\frac{\sqrt3}{2}\)，得 1 条直线。

共 3 条，且都满足与三圆交于两点。故 B 正确。

**C 选项**：取 \(b=0\)，则需 \(k^2>2\)。此时
\[
S=s_1+s_2+s_3
=\frac4{\sqrt{1+k^2}}+2\sqrt{\frac{k^2-2}{1+k^2}}.
\]
计算得
\[
S(2)>3,\qquad S(3)<3,
\]
由连续性知存在 \(k_0\in(2,3)\) 使 \(S(k_0)=3\)，由对称性也有 \(-k_0\) 对应一条。因此除 B 中 3 条外还有额外直线，故满足 \(s_1+s_2+s_3=3\) 的直线多于 3 条。C 正确。

**D 选项**：当 \(b=0\) 时，令 \(t=k^2>2\)，则
\[
S(t)=\frac{4+2\sqrt{t-2}}{\sqrt{1+t}}.
\]
令 \(u=\sqrt{t-2}>0\)，得
\[
S(u)=\frac{4+2u}{\sqrt{u^2+3}},
\]
求导得
\[
S'(u)=\frac{2(3-2u)}{(u^2+3)^{3/2}},
\]
所以 \(u=\frac32\) 时取最大值：
\[
S_{\max}=\frac{4+3}{\sqrt{\frac94+3}}
=\frac{7}{\sqrt{21/4}}
=\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}.
\]
故 D 正确。

\[
\boxed{BCD}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer in Chinese likely. Need solve multiple-choice. Need step-by-step. Need be careful.

We have three unit circles:
C1 center (-1,0), radius 1.
C2 center (1,0), radius 1.
C3 center (0, sqrt3), radius 1.

Line l: y = kx + b. It intersects each circle at two points => distance from center to line < 1 for each. Denote chord lengths s_i = 2 sqrt(1 - d_i^2), where d_i = distance from center_i to line.

Compute d_i:
For line y = kx + b -> standard kx - y + b =0? Actually y - kx - b=0. Distance from (x0,y0): |y0 - kx0 - b| / sqrt(k^2+1). Equivalent |kx0 - y0 + b| / sqrt(k^2+1). Use d_i = |k x_i - y_i + b| / sqrt(k^2+1) maybe.

Centers: O1=(-1,0), O2=(1,0), O3=(0,√3).
Numerators:
n1 = |k(-1) -0 + b| = |b - k|
n2 = |k(1) -0 + b| = |b + k|
n3 = |k*0 - √3 + b| = |b - √3|.

Need intersect two points: d_i <1 => n_i < sqrt(1+k^2).

s_i = 2 sqrt(1 - d_i^2) = 2 sqrt(1 - n_i^2/(1+k^2)) = (2 / sqrt(1+k^2)) sqrt(1+k^2 - n_i^2).

Need evaluate options.

A. k can be any real number: Is there for every k exists b such that line intersects all three circles in two points? Need examine feasible b interval for given k: |b-k|<√(1+k^2), |b+k|<√(1+k^2), |b-√3|<√(1+k^2). b interval intersection nonempty? For k any real? likely yes? Need check k large maybe? Intervals:
I1 = (k - R, k + R)
I2 = (-k - R, -k + R)

gaokao_000_011 ✓ \frac{\sqrt{66}}{6} \frac{\sqrt{66}}{6} 452 https://api.deepseek.com
Prompt
请解答以下数学题（填空题）。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

12. 双曲线5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1的离心率为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
将方程化为标准形式：

\[
5x^2-6y^2=1 \Rightarrow \frac{x^2}{\frac15}-\frac{y^2}{\frac16}=1
\]

所以

\[
a^2=\frac15,\quad b^2=\frac16
\]

双曲线中：

\[
c^2=a^2+b^2=\frac15+\frac16=\frac{11}{30}
\]

离心率：

\[
e=\frac ca=\sqrt{\frac{c^2}{a^2}}
=\sqrt{\frac{\frac{11}{30}}{\frac15}}
=\sqrt{\frac{11}{6}}
=\frac{\sqrt{66}}{6}
\]

\[
\boxed{\frac{\sqrt{66}}{6}}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer in Chinese likely. Need solve problem: 双曲线 5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1 的离心率. Need compute. Standard hyperbola x^2/a^2 - y^2/b^2 =1. Here 5x^2 -6y^2=1 => x^2/(1/5) - y^2/(1/6)=1. So a^2=1/5, b^2=1/6. For hyperbola, c^2=a^2+b^2 = 1/5+1/6=11/30. Eccentricity e=c/a = sqrt(c^2/a^2)= sqrt((11/30)/(1/5)) = sqrt(11/30 *5)= sqrt(11/6) = √66/6? Wait sqrt(11/6) = √66/6. Let's verify: a^2=1/5 => a=1/√5; c=√(11/30). e=c/a = √(11/30)*√5 = √(55/30)=√(11/6) yes. Rationalized √66/6. Need fill blank.

Need perhaps answer in \boxed{\dfrac{\sqrt{66}}{6}} or \sqrt{11/6}. Let's craft with brief reasoning and final in box.

gaokao_000_012 ✗ \frac{3\pi}{2}, 1 \theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\quad f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1 12023 https://api.deepseek.com
Prompt
请解答以下数学题（填空题）。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

13. 已知f(x)=2sin(ax+\theta) (a \in Z, 0 \le \theta < 2\pi)是偶函数，f(x)在区间(0, \frac{\pi}{2})单调递增。则\theta =__________，f(\frac{2\pi}{3}) =__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由 \(f(x)\) 为偶函数：
\[
f(-x)=f(x)
\]
即
\[
\sin(\theta-ax)=\sin(\theta+ax)
\]
利用和差化积：
\[
\sin(\theta-ax)-\sin(\theta+ax)=-2\cos\theta\sin(ax)=0
\]
若 \(a=0\)，则 \(f(x)\) 为常数，不可能单调递增，所以 \(a\ne 0\)。因此
\[
\cos\theta=0
\]
又 \(0\le \theta<2\pi\)，所以
\[
\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\quad \text{或}\quad \theta=\frac{3\pi}{2}
\]

若 \(\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\)，则
\[
f(x)=2\cos(ax)
\]
在 \(x=0\) 右侧附近减小，不满足单调递增。故
\[
\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2}
\]

此时
\[
f(x)=-2\cos(ax)
\]
要让其在 \((0,\frac{\pi}{2})\) 上单调递增，可得 \(|a|=1\) 或 \(2\)。无论 \(a=\pm1\) 还是 \(a=\pm2\)，都有
\[
f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=-2\cos\left(\frac{2a\pi}{3}\right)=1
\]

所以最终答案为：
\[
\boxed{\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\quad f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer in Chinese probably. Need solve. Need be careful. Need solve problem.

We have f(x)=2 sin(a x + theta), a integer, 0<=theta<2pi, is even function, f(x) increasing on interval (0, pi/2). Find theta and f(2pi/3).

Need analyze.

Function f(x)=2 sin(a x + θ), a ∈ Z, θ ∈ [0,2π). Even means f(-x)=f(x) for all x (assuming domain all real). sin(a(-x)+θ)=sin(-a x + θ) = sin(θ - a x). Need equal sin(a x+θ). Identity sin(u)=sin(v) with u=θ+a x, v=θ - a x. Difference u - v = 2 a x. For equality for all x? Use sin u - sin v = 2 cos((u+v)/2) sin((u-v)/2)=2 cos θ sin(a x)=0 for all x. Therefore either cos θ=0 or a=0? Wait sin(a x) zero for all x implies a=0. So even iff cosθ=0 or a=0? Let's check if a=0: f constant 2 sin θ. Even constant, okay. But monotonic increasing on (0,π/2) impossible if constant, unless a=0 yields constant not strictly? "单调递增" usually nondecreasing maybe but for constant maybe not strictly; but likely strictly? In Chinese high school, "单调递增" can mean strictly increasing? Usually "增函数" monotonic increasing means strictly increasing? Need consider. Since f(x)=2 sin(0*x+θ)=2sinθ constant, not increasing (unless "单调递增" sometimes "non-decreasing", but constant is both nondecreasing and nonincreasing but not strictly; in textbooks "单调递增" often defined: for x1<x2, f(x1)<f(x2)? Actually Chinese 高中数学: 增函数 if for任意x1<x2, f(x1)<f(x2); 减函数 if >. Non-decreasing called "单调递增"? Let's recall: In Chinese, "增函数" 定义: 设函数定义域D，如果对于定义域D内某个区间上任意两个自变量的值x1,x2，当x1<x2时，都有 f(x1)<f(x2)，则说在这个区间上是增函数. So strict. So a !=0. Then even implies cos θ=0. θ in [0,2π), so θ=π/2 or 3π/2.

Then f(x)=2 sin(a x + π/2)=2 cos(a x) if θ=π/2; or =2 sin(a x+3π/2) = -2 cos(a x) if θ=3π/2.

Need monotonic increasing on (0, π/2). Need determine θ, a? There may be multiple a? Maybe question expects unique values? Let's investigate.

Case θ=π/2: f(x)=2 cos(a x). Need increasing on (0,π/2). Derivative f'=-2 a sin(a x) > 0 for all x in (0,π/2). a integer nonzero. Need analyze depending a.

Case θ=3π/2: f(x)= -2 cos(a x). Need derivative = 2 a sin(a x) > 0 for all x in (0,π/2).

Need "f(x)在区间(0, π/2)单调递增" all? Since f periodic maybe if |a| large, on interval covers multiple periods; derivative sign not constant except perhaps a restricted. Let's solve.

```
解：
f(-x)=f(x) ⇒ sin(θ−ax)=sin(θ+ax).
由和差化积：
sin(θ−ax)−sin(θ+ax)=−2cosθ sin(ax)=0.
若a=0，f为常数，不可能严格递增，所以a≠0，故cosθ=0。
在[0,2π)内 θ=π/2 或 3π/2。

若θ=π/2，则f=2cos(ax)，在x=0附近f'(x)=-2a sin(ax)≈-2a^2x<0，故不递增。所以 θ=3π/2。
此时 f=-2cos(ax)。令m=|a|>0，则 f=-2cos(mx)，f'=2m sin(mx)。要在(0,π/2)上递增，需mπ/2≤π，即m≤2，因此m=1或2。
故 f(2π/3)=-2cos(a·2π/3)=-2cos(±2π/3 或 ±4π/3)=1。

\boxed{\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\quad f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1}
```

gaokao_000_013 ✗ \sqrt[3]{\frac{3}{2}} \sqrt[3]{\frac32} 22166 https://api.deepseek.com
Prompt
请解答以下数学题（填空题）。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

14. 设实数q满足：存在数列{a_n}，使得对于任意n \in N^*，均有a_1 + a_2 + \cdots + a_{3n} = n^2 + n，且{a_n}中有某连续9项a_k, a_{k+1}, \cdots, a_{k+8}是公比为q的等比数列。则q的最大值为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设第 \(j\) 个三项块的和为  
\[
B_j=a_{3j-2}+a_{3j-1}+a_{3j}=S_{3j}-S_{3(j-1)}=2j.
\]

若等比数列从 \(k=3m-2\) 开始，则它正好占满三个完整块 \(B_m,B_{m+1},B_{m+2}\)，三块和应为  
\[
A(1+q+q^2),\ Aq^3(1+q+q^2),\ Aq^6(1+q+q^2),
\]
分别等于 \(2m,2m+2,2m+4\)，会推出  
\[
q^3=\frac{m+1}{m}=\frac{m+2}{m+1},
\]
矛盾。

因此只能从 \(k=3m-1\) 或 \(k=3m\) 开始，此时 9 项中恰有两个完整块 \(B_{m+1},B_{m+2}\)，它们的和为 \(2m+2,2m+4\)，而等比数列中这两块和的比值为 \(q^3\)，所以  
\[
q^3=\frac{2m+4}{2m+2}=\frac{m+2}{m+1}.
\]
于是  
\[
q=\sqrt[3]{\frac{m+2}{m+1}}.
\]
该值随 \(m\) 增大而减小，故最大时取 \(m=1\)，  
\[
q_{\max}=\sqrt[3]{\frac32}.
\]

\[
\boxed{\sqrt[3]{\frac32}}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer in Chinese. Need solve problem. Let's restate.

Problem 14: Real q satisfies: there exists sequence {a_n}, such that for every n in N^* (positive integers?), we have a_1 + a2 + ... + a_{3n} = n^2 + n, and among {a_n}, some consecutive 9 terms a_k,...,a_{k+8} form geometric progression with common ratio q. Find maximum q.

We need solve. Need be careful: sequence real? "实数q" and sequence? Probably terms real. Condition: sum first 3n terms equals n^2+n for all positive integers n. This determines sums S_{3n}=n^2+n. Need sequence exists with those partial sums at multiples of 3 and contains 9-term GP ratio q. Need maximize q.

Let's denote S_m = sum_{i=1}^m a_i. Constraints: S_{3n}=n^2+n for all n>=1. No constraints on S_m for m not multiple of 3, except differences define a_i maybe arbitrary real. Existence of sequence with prescribed S_{3n} is always possible: define a_1=S_3 - S_0? Actually S_{3}=2 => a1+a2+a3=2; a4+a5+a6 = S_6 - S_3 = 6? Wait S_3=1^2+1=2; S_6=2^2+2=6; difference=4; S_9=9+3=12; diff=6; in general block from index 3(n-1)+1 to 3n sum = S_{3n}-S_{3(n-1)} = (n^2+n)-((n-1)^2+(n-1)) = n^2+n - (n^2-2n+1+n-1)= n^2+n - (n^2 - n)=2n. So block n has 3 terms sum 2n. Easy.

Need contain 9 consecutive terms a_k...a_{k+8} forming GP ratio q. Since blocks of 3 with known sums, 9 terms span exactly 3 blocks: from some block b to b+2? Let's map: k arbitrary positive integer. Let k = 3(m-1)+r where r=1,2,3? Actually positions grouped into blocks B_j = positions 3j-2,3j-1,3j sum = 2j. A 9 consecutive terms starting at k spans either:
- if r=1: exactly blocks m, m+1, m+2 (full three blocks), because 9 = 3*3.
- if r=2: positions last 2 of block m, all 3 of block m+1, all 3 of block m+2, first 1 of block m+3.
- if r=3: last 1 of block m, all 3 of block m+1,m+2,m+3, first 2 of block m+4.
But because only block sums known, not individual terms, many degrees.

We need maximize q real such that there exists sequence satisfying block sums and a 9-term GP.

Let's formalize. Let blocks indexed j>=1: x_j = a_{3j-2}, y_j = a_{3j-1}, z_j = a_{3j}; with x_j+y_j+z_j = 2j.

A 9-term GP starting at k:
Case A k=3m-2: terms x_m, y_m, z_m, x_{m+1}, y_{m+1}, z_{m+1}, x_{m+2}, y_{m+2}, z_{m+2} form GP: terms t_i = A q^{i-1}, i=1..9. Then block sums:
m block: A(1+q+q^2)=2m.
m+1: A q^3(1+q+q^2)=2(m+1).
m+2: A q^6(1+q+q^2)=2(m+2).
Thus 2(m+1)/2m = q^3 and 2(m+2)/2m = q^6. So (m+1)/m = q^3, (m+2)/m = q^6. Then q^6 = (q^3)^2? Actually [(m+1)/m]^2 = (m+2)/m => (m+1)^2 = m(m+2) => m^2+2m+1 = m^2+2m => 1=0 impossible. Wait q^6 = (m+2)/m, but q^3=(m+1)/m => q^6=(m+1)^2/m^2, must equal (m+2)/m -> contradiction. So Case A no possible? Except q? If m? No. Since ratios of block sums must be q^3 and q^6; impossible because consecutive block sums arithmetic progression 2m, 2m+2, 2m+4, not geometric with ratio q^3? Indeed need S_{m+1}/S_m = S_{m+2}/S_{m+1}; S_{m+1}/S_m=(m+1)/m, S_{m+2}/S_{m+1}=(m+2)/(m+1); equality gives (m+1)^2=m(m+2) impossible. Thus 9-term GP cannot be aligned exactly to block boundaries.

Case B k=3m-1: terms: y_m, z_m, x_{m+1}, y_{m+1}, z_{m+1}, x_{m+2}, y_{m+2}, z_{m+2}, x_{m+3}. Let GP terms t_i = A q^{i-1}, i=1..9. Then block sums:
Block m sum = t_1 + t_2 = A(1+q) = 2m. (only last two terms of block m)
Block m+1 sum = t_3+t_4+t_5 = A q^2(1+q+q^2) = 2(m+1).
Block m+2 sum = t_6+t_7+t_8 = A q^5(1+q+q^2) = 2(m+2).
Block m+3 sum = t_9 = A q^8 = 2(m+3). (only first term)
Need m>=1, q real maybe nonzero? If q=0 geometric progression with ratio 0? Typically common ratio q real, possibly 0? Consecutive terms with q=0 means after first all zero; but block m+3 sum = t_9=0 should equal 2(m+3)>0 contradiction if m>=? Actually t_9=A q^8 if q=0 =>0 not 2(m+3), impossible. So q≠0.

Equations:
(1) A(1+q)=2m.
(2) A q^2(1+q+q^2)=2m+2.
(3) A q^5(1+q+q^2)=2m+4.
(4) A q^8=2m+6.

From (2),(3): q^3 = (2m+4)/(2m+2) = (m+2)/(m+1).
From (3),(4): q^3 = (2m+6)/(2m+4)= (m+3)/(m+2).
Thus (m+2)/(m+1) = (m+3)/(m+2) => (m+2)^2 = (m+1)(m+3)=m^2+4m+3 => m^2+4m+4 = m^2+4m+3 => 4=3 contradiction. So Case B impossible? Wait check block m+3 sum = t_9? Starting positions: k=3m-1, 9 terms: indices 3m-1,3m,3m+1,3m+2,3m+3,3m+4,3m+5,3m+6,3m+7. Blocks:
Block m: positions 3m-2,3m-1,3m => includes y_m=index 3m-1, z_m=3m: t1+t2 yes sum = 2m. Good.
Block m+1: positions 3m+1,3m+2,3m+3 => t3+t4+t5 sum = 2m+2.
Block m+2: positions 3m+4,3m+5,3m+6 => t6+t7+t8 sum = 2m+4.
Block m+3: positions 3m+7,3m+8,3m+9 => only t9 = index 3m+7 sum block = 2m+6. Yes t9=2m+6. Contradiction as above.

Case C k=3m: terms: z_m, x_{m+1}, y_{m+1}, z_{m+1}, x_{m+2}, y_{m+2}, z_{m+2}, x_{m+3}, y_{m+3}. GP t_i=A q^{i-1}, i=1..9.
Block m sum = t_1 = A = 2m.
Block m+1 sum = t_2+t_3+t_4 = A(q + q^2 + q^3)= A q(1+q+q^2)=2m+2.
Block m+2 sum = t_5+t_6+t_7 = A q^4(1+q+q^2)=2m+4.
Block m+3 sum = t_8+t_9 = A(q^7+q^8)= A q^7(1+q)=2m+6.
Equations:
A=2m.
2m q(1+q+q^2)=2m+2 -> q(1+q+q^2) = (m+1)/m.
2m q^4(1+q+q^2)=2m+4 -> q^4(1+q+q^2) = (m+2)/m.
2m q^7(1+q)=2m+6 -> q^7(1+q) = (m+3)/m.
From first two: q^3 = (m+2)/(m+1). From second and third? q^3 = [(m+3)/m] / [(m+2)/m] = (m+3)/(m+2)? Wait third divided by second: [q^7(1+q)]/[q^4(1+q+q^2)] = q^3 * (1+q)/(1+q+q^2)? Actually t_8+t_9 sum = A q^7(1+q); block m+2 sum = A q^4(1+q+q^2); ratio = q^3 (1+q)/(1+q+q^2) = (m+3)/(m+2). Not simply q^3. Need use A=2m and other equations.

From first eq: q(1+q+q^2) = (m+1)/m. (E1)
Second: q^4(1+q+q^2) = q^3 * [q(1+q+q^2)] = q^3 (m+1)/m = (m+2)/m -> q^3 = (m+2)/(m+1). (E2)
Third: q^7(1+q) = q^6 * q(1+q). Need express q(1+q) maybe from E1? q(1+q+q^2)=q+q^2+q^3=(m+1)/m. q(1+q)=q+q^2. Need another.

设 \(B_j=a_{3j-2}+a_{3j-1}+a_{3j}\). 由条件 \(S_{3j}=j^2+j\), \(B_j=S_{3j}-S_{3(j-1)}=2j\).

设 9 项等比数列为 \(A, Aq,\dots,Aq^8\).
- 若起始位置 \(k=3m-2\)，则正好占完整块 \(B_m,B_{m+1},B_{m+2}\)。三块和为 \(A(1+q+q^2), Aq^3(1+q+q^2), Aq^6(1+q+q^2)\)，分别等于 \(2m,2m+2,2m+4\)，于是 \(q^3=(m+1)/m=(m+2)/(m+1)\)，矛盾。
- 因此只能 \(k=3m-1\) 或 \(3m\)，此时 9 项中恰有两个完整块 \(B_{m+1},B_{m+2}\)。它们的 GP 和分别为 \(A q^r(1+q+q^2)\) 与 \(A q^{r+3}(1+q+q^2)\)，比值 \(q^3\)，而实际块和比值为 \((2m+4)/(2m+2)=(m+2)/(m+1)\)，所以
\[
q=\sqrt[3]{\frac{m+2}{m+1}},\quad m\in\mathbb N^*.
\]
该值随 \(m\) 增大而减小，故最大在 \(m=1\)，即
\[
q_{\max}=\sqrt[3]{\frac{3}{2}}.
\]
且可以构造数列（如选择 \(k=2\)，令 \(A=4/[q^2(1+q+q^2)]\)，其余块用适当实数补足块和）实现，故可取到。

Final \(\boxed{\sqrt[3]{\frac{3}{2}}}\).
